+ "#include <metal_stdlib>\nusing namespace metal;\n\nstruct VideoEffectVertexOut {\n    float4 position [[position]];\n    float2 uv;\n};\n\n// Mirrors VideoEffectsRenderer.FrameUniforms; buffer(0) of every pass.\nstruct VideoEffectFrameUniforms {\n    float2 resolution;\n    float2 sourceSize;\n    float scaleFactor;\n    float time;\n};\n\n// Mirrors VideoEffectsRenderer.SourceUniforms. The framework's source pass only.\nstruct VideoEffectSourceUniforms {\n    float2 resolution;\n    float2 sourceSize;\n    uint gravity;\n};\n\nconstexpr sampler videoEffectSampler(coord::normalized, address::clamp_to_edge, filter::linear);\n\n// Full-screen triangle strip; `uv` is y-flipped to match CVMetalTexture.\nvertex VideoEffectVertexOut videoEffectVertex(uint vertexID [[vertex_id]]) {\n    const float2 corners[4] = {\n        float2(-1.0, -1.0), float2(1.0, -1.0), float2(-1.0, 1.0), float2(1.0, 1.0)\n    };\n    float2 corner = corners[vertexID];\n    VideoEffectVertexOut out;\n    out.position = float4(corner, 0.0, 1.0);\n    out.uv = float2(corner.x * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.5 - corner.y * 0.5);\n    return out;\n}\n\nstatic inline float videoEffectLuminance(float3 color) {\n    return dot(color, float3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));\n}\n\n// A cheap, stable value hash for per-pixel or per-row noise.\nstatic inline float videoEffectHash(float2 position) {\n    return fract(sin(dot(position, float2(12.9898, 78.233))) * 43758.5453);\n}\n\n// Aspect-fill: scales the cropped axis about the centre.\nstatic inline float2 videoEffectCoverUV(float2 uv, float2 resolution, float2 sourceSize) {\n    if (resolution.x <= 0.0 || resolution.y <= 0.0 || sourceSize.x <= 0.0 || sourceSize.y <= 0.0) {\n        return uv;\n    }\n    float viewAspect = resolution.x / resolution.y;\n    float sourceAspect = sourceSize.x / sourceSize.y;\n    float2 scale = float2(1.0);\n    if (sourceAspect > viewAspect) {\n        scale.x = viewAspect / sourceAspect;\n    } else {\n        scale.y = sourceAspect / viewAspect;\n    }\n    return (uv - 0.5) * scale + 0.5;\n}\n\n// Aspect-fit: uv leaves 0...1 in letterboxed bands, painted black.\nstatic inline float2 videoEffectContainUV(float2 uv, float2 resolution, float2 sourceSize) {\n    if (resolution.x <= 0.0 || resolution.y <= 0.0 || sourceSize.x <= 0.0 || sourceSize.y <= 0.0) {\n        return uv;\n    }\n    float viewAspect = resolution.x / resolution.y;\n    float sourceAspect = sourceSize.x / sourceSize.y;\n    float2 scale = float2(1.0);\n    if (sourceAspect > viewAspect) {\n        scale.y = sourceAspect / viewAspect;\n    } else {\n        scale.x = viewAspect / sourceAspect;\n    }\n    return (uv - 0.5) * scale + 0.5;\n}\n\n// Coverage of a band over this pixel's footprint, measured in POINTS.\nstatic inline float videoEffectBandCoverage(float positionY,\n                                            float scaleFactor,\n                                            float periodPoints,\n                                            float linePoints) {\n    float pixelPoints = (scaleFactor > 0.0) ? 1.0 / scaleFactor : 1.0;\n    float centre = positionY * pixelPoints;\n    float lo = centre - 0.5 * pixelPoints;\n    float hi = centre + 0.5 * pixelPoints;\n    float period = max(periodPoints, 1e-4);\n    float line = max(linePoints, 0.0);\n    // Period finer than the pixel footprint: the box filter is just the duty cycle.\n    if (period <= pixelPoints) {\n        return saturate(line / period);\n    }\n    // Past that point at most two consecutive periods can overlap the pixel.\n    float bandStart = floor(lo / period) * period;\n    float coverage = 0.0;\n    for (uint i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {\n        float start = bandStart + float(i) * period;\n        float end = start + line;\n        coverage += max(0.0, min(hi, end) - max(lo, start));\n    }\n    return saturate(coverage / max(hi - lo, 1e-6));\n}\n\n// Source pass: blits each source into view space with the requested gravity.\nfragment float4 videoEffectSourceFragment(VideoEffectVertexOut in [[stage_in]],\n                                          texture2d<float> source [[texture(0)]],\n                                          constant VideoEffectSourceUniforms &u [[buffer(0)]]) {\n    float2 uv = in.uv;\n    if (u.gravity == 0) {\n        uv = videoEffectContainUV(uv, u.resolution, u.sourceSize);\n        if (uv.x < 0.0 || uv.x > 1.0 || uv.y < 0.0 || uv.y > 1.0) {\n            return float4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);\n        }\n    } else if (u.gravity == 1) {\n        uv = videoEffectCoverUV(uv, u.resolution, u.sourceSize);\n    }\n    return float4(source.sample(videoEffectSampler, uv).rgb, 1.0);\n}\n"

+ "struct CrossfadeVideoEffectUniforms {\n    float progress;\n};\n\nfragment float4 crossfadeVideoEffectFragment(VideoEffectVertexOut in [[stage_in]],\n                                             texture2d<float> image [[texture(0)]],\n                                             texture2d<float> incoming [[texture(1)]],\n                                             constant VideoEffectFrameUniforms &frame [[buffer(0)]],\n                                             constant CrossfadeVideoEffectUniforms &u [[buffer(1)]]) {\n    float3 outgoing = image.sample(videoEffectSampler, in.uv).rgb;\n    float3 arriving = incoming.sample(videoEffectSampler, in.uv).rgb;\n    return float4(mix(outgoing, arriving, saturate(u.progress)), 1.0);\n}"
+ "struct ScanlineVideoEffectUniforms {\n    float opacity;\n    float periodPoints;\n    float linePoints;\n    float lineAlpha;\n};\n\nfragment float4 scanlineVideoEffectFragment(VideoEffectVertexOut in [[stage_in]],\n                                            texture2d<float> image [[texture(0)]],\n                                            constant VideoEffectFrameUniforms &frame [[buffer(0)]],\n                                            constant ScanlineVideoEffectUniforms &u [[buffer(1)]]) {\n    float3 color = image.sample(videoEffectSampler, in.uv).rgb;\n    float coverage = videoEffectBandCoverage(in.position.y,\n                                             frame.scaleFactor,\n                                             u.periodPoints,\n                                             u.linePoints);\n    float alpha = coverage * u.lineAlpha * u.opacity;\n    return float4(mix(color, float3(1.0), alpha), 1.0);\n}"
+ "struct SliceGlitchVideoEffectUniforms {\n    float opacity;\n    uint rowCount;\n};\n\nfragment float4 sliceGlitchVideoEffectFragment(VideoEffectVertexOut in [[stage_in]],\n                                               texture2d<float> image [[texture(0)]],\n                                               constant VideoEffectFrameUniforms &frame [[buffer(0)]],\n                                               constant SliceGlitchVideoEffectUniforms &u [[buffer(1)]],\n                                               constant float *rowOffsets [[buffer(2)]]) {\n    float3 clean = image.sample(videoEffectSampler, in.uv).rgb;\n    if (u.opacity <= 0.0 || u.rowCount == 0) {\n        return float4(clean, 1.0);\n    }\n    uint row = min(uint(max(in.position.y, 0.0)), u.rowCount - 1);\n    float shifted = in.uv.x + rowOffsets[row];\n    if (shifted < 0.0 || shifted >= 1.0) {\n        return float4(clean, 1.0);\n    }\n    float3 corrupted = image.sample(videoEffectSampler, float2(shifted, in.uv.y)).rgb;\n    return float4(mix(clean, corrupted, u.opacity), 1.0);\n}"
